  43:     private final PositionPriceAuthorityPolicy priceAuthorityPolicy;
  44:     private final PositionThesisPressurePolicy thesisPressurePolicy;
  45: 
  46:     @Transactional
  47:     public Optional<PositionAnalysis> analyze(TradeSignal signal) {
  48:         if (!validSignal(signal)) return Optional.empty();
  49: 
  50:         String symbol = signal.getSymbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  51:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
  52:                 .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  53:                 .orElse(null);
  54:         if (!validPosition(position)) return Optional.empty();
  55: 
  56:         if (analysisRepository.existsByWalletPositionIdAndTradeSignalId(position.getId(), signal.getId())) {
  57:             return analysisRepository.findTopByWalletPositionIdOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc(position.getId());
  58:         }
  59: 
  60:         BigDecimal entry = nvl(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
  61:         BigDecimal price = signal.getLatestPrice();
  62:         BigDecimal pnl = price.subtract(entry).multiply(position.getQuantity());
  63:         BigDecimal pnlPercent = percentageChange(entry, price);
  64:         long holdingMinutes = Math.max(0,
  65:                 Duration.between(position.getOpenedAt(), Instant.now()).toMinutes());
  66: 
  67:         // A TradeSignal price can belong to a candle that closed BEFORE this position opened.
  68:         // ALLOUSDT incident 2026-08-17: a delayed 5m signal carried 0.2806 from the
  69:         // 18:15-18:19 candle and falsely stopped a position opened at 18:23 @ 0.2822.
  70:         // Such signals are still valid thesis/MTF context, but they are forbidden from
  71:         // driving price-based hard protection. Live TP/SL is handled by
  72:         // LivePositionProtectionService from the actual live Binance price stream.
  73:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, position.getOpenedAt());
  74:         PositionRecommendation hardRecommendation = authoritativePrice
  75:                 ? hardRiskRecommendation(position, price)
  76:                 : null;
  77: 
  78:         PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis = thesisPressurePolicy.evaluate(
  79:                 position.getEntryTrendScore(), position.getEntryStructureScore(),
  80:                 position.getEntryMomentumScore(), position.getEntryVolumeScore(),
  81:                 position.getEntryConfidence(), position.getEntryTotalScore(), signal);
  82:         int trendDeterioration = thesis.trendPressure();
  83:         int momentumExhaustion = thesis.momentumPressure();
  84:         int profitProtection = profitProtection(pnlPercent, thesis);
  85:         int riskEvents = authoritativePrice ? stopRiskPressure(position, price) : 0;
  86:         int opportunityCost = 0; // intentionally excluded from v1
  87:         int exitScore = Math.min(25, trendDeterioration + momentumExhaustion
  88:                 + profitProtection + riskEvents);
  89: 
  90:         int authority = timeframeAuthority(signal.getInterval());
  91:         PositionRecommendation recommendation = hardRecommendation != null
  92:                 ? hardRecommendation
  93:                 : recommendation(exitScore, profitProtection, pnlPercent, authority);
  94:         int confidence = confidence(exitScore, recommendation, hardRecommendation != null);
  95: 
  96:         List<String> evidence = evidence(position, signal, pnlPercent, thesis,
  97:                 trendDeterioration, momentumExhaustion, profitProtection,
  98:                 riskEvents, authority, hardRecommendation);
  99:         String explanation = recommendation + " advisory: " + String.join("; ", evidence);
 100: 
 101:         Map<String, Object> details = details(position, signal, pnlPercent, thesis,
 102:                 trendDeterioration, momentumExhaustion, profitProtection,
 103:                 riskEvents, opportunityCost, exitScore, authority, evidence);
 104: 
 105:         PositionAnalysis saved = analysisRepository.save(PositionAnalysis.builder()
 106:                 .walletPosition(position)
 107:                 .tradeSignal(signal)
 108:                 .symbol(symbol)
 109:                 .intervalCode(signal.getInterval())
 110:                 .entryPriceUsdt(entry)
 111:                 .currentPriceUsdt(price)
 112:                 .unrealizedPnlUsdt(pnl)
 113:                 .unrealizedPnlPercent(pnlPercent)
 114:                 .holdingMinutes(holdingMinutes)
 115:                 .trendDeteriorationScore(trendDeterioration)
 116:                 .momentumExhaustionScore(momentumExhaustion)
 117:                 .profitProtectionScore(profitProtection)
 118:                 .riskEventScore(riskEvents)
 119:                 .opportunityCostScore(opportunityCost)
 120:                 .exitScore(exitScore)
 121:                 .recommendation(recommendation)
 122:                 .confidence(confidence)
 123:                 .explanation(limit(explanation, 2000))
 124:                 .detailsJson(toJson(details))
 125:                 .advisoryOnly(true)
 126:                 .analyzedAt(Instant.now())
 127:                 .build());
 128: 
 129:         if (recommendation == PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS) {
 130:             boolean applied = walletAutoExecutionService.executePositionStopLoss(saved);
 131:             if (applied) {
 132:                 saved.setAdvisoryOnly(false);
 133:                 details.put("advisoryOnly", false);
 134:                 details.put("walletApplied", true);
 135:                 details.put("walletExecutionReason", "POSITION_STOP_LOSS");
 136:                 saved.setDetailsJson(toJson(details));
 137:                 saved = analysisRepository.save(saved);
 138:                 log.warn("Position STOP_LOSS applied to wallet: position={}, analysis={}, signal={}, symbol={}",
 139:                         position.getId(), saved.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol);
 140:             } else {
 141:                 log.error("Position STOP_LOSS was generated but not applied to wallet: position={}, analysis={}, signal={}, symbol={}",
 142:                         position.getId(), saved.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol);
 143:             }
 144:         }
 145: 
 146:         log.info("Position analysis created: position={}, signal={}, symbol={}, recommendation={}, exitScore={}/25, advisoryOnly={}",
 147:                 position.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol, recommendation, exitScore, saved.isAdvisoryOnly());
 148:         return Optional.of(saved);
 149:     }
 150: 
 151:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 152:     public List<PositionAnalysisView> latest() {
 153:         return analysisRepository.findTop100ByOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc().stream()
 154:                 .map(this::view)
 155:                 .toList();
 156:     }
 157: 
 158:     private boolean validSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 159:         return signal != null && signal.getId() != null && signal.getSymbol() != null
 160:                 && signal.getLatestPrice() != null && signal.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0;
 161:     }
 162: 
 163:     private boolean validPosition(WalletManagedPosition position) {
 164:         return position != null && position.getId() != null && position.getQuantity() != null
 165:                 && position.getQuantity().signum() > 0;
 166:     }
 167: 
 168:     private PositionRecommendation hardRiskRecommendation(WalletManagedPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 169:         if (p.getStopLossUsdt() != null && p.getStopLossUsdt().signum() > 0
 170:                 && price.compareTo(p.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
 171:             return PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS;
 172:         }
 173:         if (p.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && p.getTakeProfitUsdt().signum() > 0
 174:                 && price.compareTo(p.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {
 175:             return PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT;
 176:         }
 177:         return null;
 178:     }
 179: 
 180:     private int profitProtection(BigDecimal pnlPercent, PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis) {
 181:         if (pnlPercent.signum() <= 0) return 0;
 182:         int score = 0;
 183:         if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(5)) >= 0) score += 2;
 184:         else if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(2)) >= 0) score += 1;
 185:         if (thesis.trendDrop() >= 6 || thesis.structureDrop() >= 3) score += 2;
 186:         if (thesis.momentumDrop() >= 5 || thesis.totalDrop() >= 15) score += 1;
 187:         return Math.min(5, score);
 188:     }
 189: 
 190:     private int stopRiskPressure(WalletManagedPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 191:         BigDecimal stop = p.getStopLossUsdt();
 192:         BigDecimal entry = p.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt();
 193:         if (stop == null || stop.signum() <= 0 || entry == null || entry.compareTo(stop) <= 0) return 0;
 194:         BigDecimal totalRoom = entry.subtract(stop);
 195:         BigDecimal remaining = price.subtract(stop);
 196:         if (remaining.signum() <= 0) return 4;
 197:         BigDecimal ratio = remaining.divide(totalRoom, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 198:         if (ratio.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.25)) <= 0) return 3;
 199:         if (ratio.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50)) <= 0) return 1;
 200:         return 0;
 201:     }
 202: 
 203:     private PositionRecommendation recommendation(int exitScore, int profitProtection,
 204:                                                   BigDecimal pnlPercent, int authority) {
 205:         // A 1m advisory can warn or recommend reduction, but cannot independently
 206:         // invalidate the full position thesis.
 207:         if (authority >= 2 && exitScore >= 17) return PositionRecommendation.EXIT;
 208:         if (authority >= 2 && pnlPercent.signum() > 0
 209:                 && profitProtection >= 4 && exitScore >= 10) {
 210:             return PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT;
 211:         }
 212:         if (exitScore >= 10) return PositionRecommendation.REDUCE;
 213:         return PositionRecommendation.HOLD;
 214:     }
 215: 
 216:     private int timeframeAuthority(String interval) {
 217:         if (interval == null) return 1;
 218:         return switch (interval.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
 219:             case "1h", "4h", "1d" -> 3;
 220:             case "5m", "15m", "30m" -> 2;
 221:             default -> 1;
 222:         };
 223:     }
 224: 
 225:     private int confidence(int score, PositionRecommendation recommendation, boolean hardTrigger) {
 226:         if (hardTrigger) return 95;
 227:         int base = switch (recommendation) {
 228:             case HOLD -> 85 - Math.min(30, score * 3);
 229:             case REDUCE -> 65 + Math.min(20, Math.abs(score - 13) * 3);
 230:             case TAKE_PROFIT -> 75 + Math.min(15, score);
 231:             case STOP_LOSS -> 95;
 232:             case EXIT -> 75 + Math.min(20, score - 16);
 233:         };
 234:         return Math.max(50, Math.min(95, base));
 235:     }
 236: 
 237:     private List<String> evidence(WalletManagedPosition p, TradeSignal s, BigDecimal pnlPct,
 238:                                   PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure t, int trend, int momentum, int protection,
 239:                                   int risk, int authority, PositionRecommendation hard) {
 240:         List<String> e = new ArrayList<>();
 241:         e.add("Unrealized P/L is " + pnlPct.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + "%");
 242:         if (hard == PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS) {
 243:             e.add("Current price reached the immutable position stop loss " + p.getStopLossUsdt());
 244:             return e;
 245:         }
 246:         if (hard == PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT) {
 247:             e.add("Current price reached the immutable position take-profit target " + p.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 248:             return e;
 249:         }
 250:         e.add("Trend changed " + t.entryTrend() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentTrend()
 251:                 + " and structure " + t.entryStructure() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentStructure());
 252:         e.add("Momentum changed " + t.entryMomentum() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentMomentum()
 253:                 + " and volume " + t.entryVolume() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentVolume());
 254:         e.add("Total score changed " + t.entryTotal() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentTotal()
 255:                 + "; confidence " + t.entryConfidence() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentConfidence());
 256:         e.add("Thesis pressure: trend " + trend + "/8, momentum " + momentum + "/5");
 257:         if (protection > 0) e.add("Profit-protection pressure " + protection + "/5");
 258:         if (risk > 0) e.add("Price is approaching the stored stop-loss; risk pressure " + risk + "/4");
 259:         e.add("Timeframe authority " + authority + "/3 for " + s.getInterval());
 260:         if (trend <= 2 && momentum <= 1) e.add("Original BUY thesis remains broadly intact");
 261:         return e;
 262:     }
 263: 
 264:     private Map<String, Object> details(WalletManagedPosition p, TradeSignal s,
 265:                                         BigDecimal pnlPct, PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure t,
 266:                                         int trend, int momentum, int protection,
 267:                                         int risk, int opportunity, int exitScore,
 268:                                         int authority, List<String> evidence) {
 269:         Map<String, Object> details = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 270:         details.put("advisoryOnly", true);
 271:         details.put("method", "IMMUTABLE_BUY_THESIS_COMPARISON");
 272:         details.put("entrySignalId", p.getEntrySignalId());
 273:         details.put("entryDecision", p.getEntryDecision());
 274:         details.put("entryPriceUsdt", p.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
 275:         details.put("stopLossUsdt", p.getStopLossUsdt());
 276:         details.put("takeProfitUsdt", p.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 277:         details.put("currentPriceUsdt", s.getLatestPrice());
 278:         details.put("unrealizedPnlPercent", pnlPct);
 279:         details.put("timeframeAuthority", authority);
 280:         details.put("entry", Map.of(

  13: /**
  14:  * FIX-052 exact Replay/Production parity support.
  15:  *
  16:  * Production protects open positions from every canonical 1m Binance live-price
  17:  * update, not only from candle-close analysis signals. Persisting that exact feed
  18:  * lets Replay evaluate TP/SL/profit-lock against the same observations and order.
  19:  * Timestamps are always stored/interpreted as UTC Instants; timezone conversion is
  20:  * a presentation concern only.
  21:  */
  22: @Service
  23: @org.springframework.context.annotation.DependsOn("localTraderInstance")
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: public class MarketPriceEventService {
  26:     private static final int PRICE_SCALE=12;
  27:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  28:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  29: 
  30:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode="OFF";
  31:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${local-candle.price-max-age-seconds:15}") private long maxAge=15;
  32:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${local-candle.price-future-skew-seconds:2}") private long futureSkew=2;
  33:     private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(MarketPriceEventService.class);
  34:     private final java.util.Set<Long> inFlight=java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  35:     public static final String DIRECT_SOURCE="BINANCE_KLINE_DIRECT_FIX153";
  36:     public static BigDecimal normalizePrice(BigDecimal price){
  37:         return price==null?null:price.setScale(PRICE_SCALE,java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
  38:     }
  39:     private Instant referenceNow(){Instant local=Instant.now();return exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);}
  40:     public String invalidReason(BigDecimal price,Instant observed,Instant now){
  41:         if(price==null || price.signum()<=0)return "INVALID_PRICE";
  42:         if(observed==null)return "MISSING_TIMESTAMP";
  43:         if(observed.isAfter(now.plusSeconds(futureSkew)))return "FUTURE_TIMESTAMP";
  44:         if(observed.isBefore(now.minusSeconds(maxAge)))return "STALE_PRICE";
  45:         return null;
  46:     }
  47:     /** FIX-153: called in INPUT transaction. Invalid observations never occupy slot 1.
  48:      * A duplicate-key error is classified only if the exact canonical key exists; other
  49:      * duplicate constraints/errors propagate. JDBC statement errors do not poison a JPA proxy TX. */
  50:     public Long recordPending(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observed){
  51:         BigDecimal normalizedPrice=normalizePrice(price);
  52:         Instant now=referenceNow();String invalid=invalidReason(normalizedPrice,observed,now);
  53:         if(invalid!=null){
  54:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);
  55:             Long ageMs=observed==null?null:java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis();
  56:             log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_NOT_CANONICAL] symbol={}, price={}, observedUtc={}, observationAgeMs={}, reason={}",symbol,normalizedPrice,observed,ageMs,invalid);
  57:             return null;
  58:         }
  59:         org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder keys=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();
  60:         try {
  61:             jdbcTemplate.update(connection->{
  62:                 var ps=connection.prepareStatement("INSERT INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,delivery_status,ordering_slot) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'PENDING',1)",new String[]{"id"});
  63:                 ps.setString(1,symbol);ps.setTimestamp(2,Timestamp.from(observed));ps.setBigDecimal(3,normalizedPrice);ps.setString(4,DIRECT_SOURCE);return ps;
  64:             },keys);
  65:             long id=java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(keys.getKey(),"generated price id").longValue();inFlight.add(id);return id;
  66:         }catch(org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate){
  67:             var first=jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id,price FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND ordering_slot=1",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed));
  68:             if(first.size()!=1)throw duplicate;
  69:             BigDecimal existing=(BigDecimal)first.getFirst().get("price");
  70:             log.warn("[FIX-153][EQUAL_TIMESTAMP_REJECTED] symbol={}, observedUtc={}, firstEvent={}, firstPrice={}, rejectedPrice={}, observationAgeMs={}, priceDisagreement={}",symbol,observed,first.getFirst().get("id"),existing,normalizedPrice,java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis(),existing.compareTo(normalizedPrice)!=0);
  71:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,delivery_status,ordering_slot,outcome_reason) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'NON_CANONICAL',NULL,?)",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),normalizedPrice,DIRECT_SOURCE,existing.compareTo(normalizedPrice)==0?"DUPLICATE_TIMESTAMP":"AMBIGUOUS_EQUAL_TIMESTAMP");

  75:     public void release(long id){inFlight.remove(id);}
  76:     /** Runs after existing symbol/position lock at READ_COMMITTED, never counts unresolved receipts. */
  77:     public boolean laterApplied(String symbol,Instant observed){
  78:         return !jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at>? AND source IN ('BINANCE_KLINE_LIVE_CLOSE',?) AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') ORDER BY observed_at DESC LIMIT 1",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),DIRECT_SOURCE).isEmpty();
  79:     }
  80:     /** Exact event lineage prevents a caller from completing a different receipt.
  81:      * A lost resolver race rolls back the entire effects transaction. Slot 1 is retained. */
  82:     public void finish(long id,String symbol,Instant observed,BigDecimal price,String state,String reason){
  83:         price=normalizePrice(price);
  84:         if(!org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
  85:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome requires effects transaction");
  86:         if(!java.util.Set.of("APPLIED","NON_CANONICAL").contains(state))throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 invalid effects outcome");
  87:         int n=jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status=?,outcome_reason=? WHERE id=? AND source=? AND symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND price=? AND ordering_slot=1 AND delivery_status='PENDING'",state,reason,id,DIRECT_SOURCE,symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),price);
  88:         if(n!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome ownership/lineage changed: event="+id);
  89:         org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization(){
  90:             @Override public void afterCompletion(int status){
  91:                 if(status==STATUS_COMMITTED){
  92:                     com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count("APPLIED".equals(state)?com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_APPLIED:com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);
  93:                     if("APPLIED".equals(state))com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.protection(symbol,observed);
  94:                     log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_OUTCOME_COMMITTED] event={}, symbol={}, observedUtc={}, observedKsa={}, outcome={}, reason={}",id,symbol,observed,observed.atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)),state,reason);
  95:                 }else log.debug("[FIX-153][PRICE_OUTCOME_NOT_COMMITTED] event={}, symbol={}, proposedOutcome={}, transactionCompletion={}; durable outcome must be read",id,symbol,state,status);
  96:             }
  97:         });
  98:     }
  99:     /** Startup and periodic conditional resolution: no replay and no symbol-entry fence.
 100:      * The conditional update waits behind an in-flight effect TX and cannot override APPLIED.
 101:      * Runtime absence alone is insufficient; candidates must also exceed a configured bound. */
 102:     @org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled(initialDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-initial-delay-ms:5000}",fixedDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-delay-ms:5000}")
 103:     public void resolvePending(){
 104:         if(!"OFF".equals(mode))return;
 105:         try {
 106:             // FIX-153: housekeeping uses its own statement timeout, without mutating the shared template.
 107:             var bounded=new JdbcTemplate(java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(jdbcTemplate.getDataSource()));bounded.setQueryTimeout(2);
 108:             long deadline=System.nanoTime()+java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);
 109:             var rows=bounded.queryForList("SELECT id,symbol,observed_at FROM market_price_event WHERE source=? AND delivery_status='PENDING' AND created_at<? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 100",DIRECT_SOURCE,Timestamp.from(Instant.now().minusSeconds(120)));
 110:             for(var row:rows){if(System.nanoTime()>deadline)break;long id=((Number)row.get("id")).longValue();if(inFlight.contains(id))continue;
 111:                 int n=bounded.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status='PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED',outcome_reason='AUTOMATIC_ATOMIC_NO_COMMIT' WHERE id=? AND source=? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",id,DIRECT_SOURCE);
 112:                 if(n==1){com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_PENDING_RESOLVED);
 113:                     log.info("[FIX-153][PENDING_RESOLVED] event={}, symbol={}, observedUtc={}, outcome=PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED, evidence=ATOMIC_EFFECTS_AND_STATUS_NO_COMMIT, replay=false",id,row.get("symbol"),row.get("observed_at"));}
 114:             }
 115:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){log.warn("[FIX-153][PENDING_RESOLUTION_FAILED] durable outcome unchanged; no live fence introduced",failure);}
 116:     }

 118:     public List<PriceEvent> find(String rawSymbol, Instant startInclusive, Instant endInclusive) {
 119:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 120:         return jdbcTemplate.query("""
 121:                 SELECT observed_at, price, source
 122:                 FROM market_price_event
 123:                 WHERE delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') AND symbol = ? AND observed_at >= ? AND observed_at <= ?
 124:                 ORDER BY observed_at ASC, id ASC
 125:                 """, (rs, rowNum) -> new PriceEvent(
 126:                         rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
 127:                         rs.getBigDecimal("price"), rs.getString("source")),
 128:                 symbol, Timestamp.from(startInclusive), Timestamp.from(endInclusive));
 129:     }

 134:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);

 137:                 FROM market_price_event

 139:                 ORDER BY observed_at DESC, id DESC
 140:                 LIMIT 1

 142:                         rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
 143:                         rs.getBigDecimal("price"), rs.getString("source")),

 148:     /** FIX-132: known skipped/unconfirmed collector delivery must not silently
 149:      * become legacy candle-price fallback. Historical pre-collector windows retain
 150:      * their established fallback. This reads immutable evidence, never live claims. */
 151:     public void assertReplayEvidence(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
 152:         Long pending=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",
 153:             Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 154:         if(pending!=null && pending>0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 Replay contains unconfirmed Production price outcomes; reconcile before claiming parity");
 155:         // FIX-153 OFF Replay never queries collector-era activation tables.
 156:         if("OFF".equals(mode))return;
 157:         Long cutovers=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at<=?",Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(to));
 158:         if(cutovers!=null && cutovers>0) {
 159:             Long applied=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED')",
 160:                 Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 161:             if(applied==null || applied==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 no applied price evidence in collector-era Replay window; signal-price fallback is not Production reproduction");
 162:         }
 163:     }
 164: 
 165:     /** FIX-153: report excluded evidence explicitly; this never promotes it to a Replay price. */
 166:     public java.util.Map<String,Long> replayOutcomeCounts(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to){
 167:         java.util.Map<String,Long> result=new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
 168:         jdbcTemplate.query("SELECT delivery_status,COUNT(*) AS n FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? GROUP BY delivery_status",
 169:             (org.springframework.jdbc.core.RowCallbackHandler)rs->result.put(rs.getString(1),rs.getLong(2)),symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 170:         return result;
 171:     }
 172: 
 173:     public record PriceEvent(Instant observedAt, BigDecimal price, String source) {
 174:         public PriceEvent(Instant observedAt,BigDecimal price) { this(observedAt,price,"BINANCE_KLINE_LIVE_CLOSE"); }
 175:     }
 176: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java =====
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.indicator.event.CandleClosedEvent;
   4: import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
   5: import com.crypto.position.service.LivePositionProtectionService;
   6: import com.crypto.debug.monitor.service.PriceMoveMonitorService;
   7: import com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService;
   8: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
   9: 
  10: import org.springframework.context.ApplicationEventPublisher;
  11: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  12: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
  13: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation;
  14: import com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.KlineTransactionCoordinator;
  15: 
  16: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  17: import java.time.Instant;
  18: import java.util.Locale;
  19: 
  20: @Service
  21: public class BinanceKlineService {
  22:     private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(BinanceKlineService.class);
  23:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  24:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  25: 
  26:     private final KlineTransactionCoordinator transactions;
  27:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  28:     private final ApplicationEventPublisher eventPublisher;
  29:     private final LivePositionProtectionService livePositionProtectionService;
  30:     private final PriceMoveMonitorService priceMoveMonitorService;
  31:     private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
  32:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false)
  33:     private com.crypto.local.LocalDrainService drain;
  34:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false)
  35:     private com.crypto.local.LocalReconciliationAuditRepository reconciliationAudit;
  36: 
  37:     public BinanceKlineService(
  38:             CandleRepository candleRepository,
  39:             ApplicationEventPublisher eventPublisher,
  40:             LivePositionProtectionService livePositionProtectionService,
  41:             PriceMoveMonitorService priceMoveMonitorService,
  42:             MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService,
  43:             KlineTransactionCoordinator transactions
  44:     ) {
  45:         this.transactions = transactions;
  46:         this.candleRepository = candleRepository;
  47:         this.eventPublisher = eventPublisher;
  48:         this.livePositionProtectionService = livePositionProtectionService;
  49:         this.priceMoveMonitorService = priceMoveMonitorService;
  50:         this.marketPriceEventService = marketPriceEventService;
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     // FIX-124: no suspended caller transaction may retain market-data locks.
  54:     @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.NEVER)
  55:     public boolean processKline(JsonNode root) {
  56:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: Trader kline ingestion is disabled"); }
  57:         if(drain!=null && !drain.beginInput()){log.info("[FIX-153][KLINE_REJECTED_DRAINING]");return false;}
  58:         try {
  59: 
  60:         JsonNode data = root.has("data")
  61:                 ? root.path("data")
  62:                 : root;
  63: 
  64:         JsonNode kline = data.path("k");
  65: 
  66:         if (kline.isMissingNode() || kline.isNull()) {
  67:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
  68:                     "Binance message does not contain kline data"
  69:             );
  70:         }
  71: 
  72:         String symbol = kline.path("s")
  73:                 .asText()
  74:                 .trim()
  75:                 .toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  76: 
  77:         String intervalCode = kline.path("i")
  78:                 .asText()
  79:                 .trim();
  80: 
  81:         Instant openTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
  82:                 kline.path("t").asLong()
  83:         );
  84: 
  85:         Instant closeTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
  86:                 kline.path("T").asLong()
  87:         );
  88: 
  89:         boolean closed = kline.path("x")
  90:                 .asBoolean(false);
  91:         BigDecimal livePrice = MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(decimal(kline, "c"));
  92: 
  93:         // FIX-153: an absent Binance event timestamp is not equivalent to a fresh receipt.
  94:         Instant observedAt = data.path("E").asLong(0L)>0 ? Instant.ofEpochMilli(data.path("E").asLong()) : null;
  95:         boolean canonical="1m".equals(intervalCode);
  96:         String token=java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
  97:         java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<CandleRepository.Transition> transition=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
  98:         java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<Long> priceId=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
  99:         long started=System.nanoTime();
 100:         try {
 101:             transactions.process(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,livePrice,
 102:                 ()->{
 103:                     transition.set(candleRepository.upsertRuntime(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,
 104:                         decimal(kline,"o"),decimal(kline,"h"),decimal(kline,"l"),livePrice,
 105:                         decimal(kline,"v"),decimal(kline,"q"),kline.path("n").asLong(),decimal(kline,"V"),decimal(kline,"Q"),closed,token));
 106:                     if(canonical)priceId.set(marketPriceEventService.recordPending(symbol,livePrice,observedAt));
 107:                 },
 108:                 canonical ? ()->{if(priceId.get()!=null)livePositionProtectionService.onPrice(symbol,livePrice,observedAt,priceId.get());} : null,
 109:                 canonical ? ()->{if(priceId.get()!=null)priceMoveMonitorService.onPrice(symbol,livePrice,observedAt);} : null,
 110:                 closed ? ()->{var result=transition.get();if(result!=null && result.transitionedToClosed())
 111:                     eventPublisher.publishEvent(new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,result.closeTime()));} : null);
 112: 
 113:             var completedTransition=transition.get();
 114:             if(closed && completedTransition!=null && !completedTransition.transitionedToClosed()) {
 115:                 com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DUPLICATE_CLOSE);
 116:                 log.info("[FIX-153][DUPLICATE_CLOSE] receipt={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}, existingToken={}",
 117:                     token,symbol,intervalCode,openTime,completedTransition.token());
 118:                 // This audit is intentionally after the ingestion transaction and
 119:                 // fail-safe: an audit problem must never make a Binance receipt fail.
 120:                 if(reconciliationAudit!=null)try {reconciliationAudit.markLateLiveClose(symbol,intervalCode,openTime);}
 121:                 catch(RuntimeException ignored){log.warn("[FIX-153][LATE_LIVE_CLOSE_AUDIT_FAILED] symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}",symbol,intervalCode,openTime);}
 122:             } else if(!closed && completedTransition!=null && completedTransition.alreadyClosed()) {
 123:                 com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.LATE_OPEN_IGNORED);
 124:                 log.info("[FIX-153][LATE_OPEN_IGNORED] receipt={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}",token,symbol,intervalCode,openTime);
 125:             }
 126: 
 127:             log.debug("[FIX-153][KLINE_PHASES_RETURNED] receipt={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}, observedUtc={}, transitionedToClosed={}, elapsedIncludingTransactionsMs={}; protection outcome is in price evidence",
 128:                 token,priceId.get(),symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,transition.get()!=null&&transition.get().transitionedToClosed(),(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000);
 129:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){
 130:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PIPELINE_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED);
 131:             log.error("[FIX-153][KLINE_PHASE_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED] receipt={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}; inspect durable evidence, do not infer no effects",token,priceId.get(),symbol,intervalCode,openTime,failure);
 132:             throw failure;
 133:         }finally{if(priceId.get()!=null)marketPriceEventService.release(priceId.get());}
 134: 
 135:         return closed;
 136:         } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeInput();}
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     private BigDecimal decimal(
 140:             JsonNode node,
 141:             String field
 142:     ) {
 143:         String value = node.path(field).asText();
 144: 
 145:         if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
 146:             return BigDecimal.ZERO;
 147:         }
 148: 
 149:         return new BigDecimal(value);
 150:     }
 151: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionExitPolicy.java:1-160 =====
1: package com.crypto.position.service;
2: 
3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
6: 
7: /**
8:  * Shared production/replay exit authority for an already-open long position.
9:  *
10:  * The policy deliberately separates short-timeframe noise from a genuine
11:  * higher-timeframe breakdown:
12:  *  - a 1m pullback alone never closes the position;
13:  *  - the existing 1m SELL + bearish 5m confirmation remains valid;
14:  *  - a fresh 1h SELL may close a position when 5m is no longer supportive;
15:  *  - a Profit Lock breach is tolerated while 5m/1h still support the bullish thesis.
16:  */
17: @Component
18: public class PositionExitPolicy {
19: 
20:     public Evaluation evaluateNormalExit(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
21:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
22:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
23:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
24: 
25:         boolean oneHourBreakdown = bearish(oneDecision)
26:                 && fiveMinute != null
27:                 && !supportive(fiveDecision);
28:         if (oneHourBreakdown) {
29:             return Evaluation.exit("HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED",
30:                     "1h turned " + label(oneDecision)
31:                             + " while 5m is no longer supportive (" + label(fiveDecision)
32:                             + "). The higher-timeframe bullish thesis is broken; close the managed position.");
33:         }
34: 
35:         boolean lowerFrameConfirmed = current != null
36:                 && "1m".equals(current.getInterval())
37:                 && bearish(currentDecision)
38:                 && fiveMinute != null
39:                 && bearish(fiveDecision)
40:                 && (oneHour == null || !bullish(oneDecision));
41:         if (lowerFrameConfirmed) {
42:             return Evaluation.exit("SELL_CONFIRMED",
43:                     "1m " + label(currentDecision) + " is confirmed by bearish 5m while 1h is not bullish.");
44:         }
45: 
46:         return Evaluation.hold("No confirmed multi-timeframe exit."
47:                 + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
48:                 + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
49:                 + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
50:     }
51: 
52:     public Evaluation evaluateProfitLockBreach(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
53:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
54:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
55:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
56: 
57:         // Strong HTF support: tolerate a sharp 1m pullback instead of throwing away the trend.
58:         if (bullish(oneDecision) && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)) {
59:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
60:                     "Profit Lock was breached, but higher-timeframe continuation remains healthy:"
61:                             + " 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
62:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
63:                             + ". Treat the breach as a protected pullback, not an immediate SELL.");
64:         }
65: 
66:         // Cooling but still-supported trend: WATCH/WATCH is allowed to breathe while the current frame is not bearish.
67:         if (oneDecision == SignalDecision.WATCH
68:                 && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)
69:                 && !bearish(currentDecision)) {
70:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
71:                     "Profit Lock was breached during a supported consolidation:"
72:                             + " 1h=WATCH, 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
73:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
74:                             + ". Keep the winner protected while waiting for higher-timeframe confirmation.");
75:         }
76: 
77:         if (bearish(oneDecision)) {
78:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_HTF_EXIT",
79:                     "Profit Lock was breached and 1h is " + label(oneDecision)
80:                             + "; higher-timeframe support has failed.");
81:         }
82: 
83:         if (bearish(currentDecision) && bearish(fiveDecision)) {
84:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_BEARISH_CONFIRMATION",
85:                     "Profit Lock was breached with bearish 1m and 5m confirmation.");
86:         }
87: 
88:         return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT",
89:                 "Profit Lock was breached without enough higher-timeframe bullish support to justify a protected hold:"
90:                         + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
91:                         + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
92:                         + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
93:     }
94: 
95:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal signal) {
96:         return signal == null ? null : signal.getDecision();
97:     }
98: 
99:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision decision) {
100:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY
101:                 || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY
102:                 || decision == SignalDecision.WATCH;
103:     }
104: 
105:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision decision) {
106:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
107:     }
108: 
109:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision decision) {
110:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
111:     }
112: 
113:     private String label(SignalDecision decision) {
114:         return decision == null ? "MISSING" : decision.name();
115:     }
116: 
117:     public record Evaluation(boolean exit, String code, String explanation) {
118:         public static Evaluation exit(String code, String explanation) {
119:             return new Evaluation(true, code, explanation);
120:         }
121: 
122:         public static Evaluation hold(String explanation) {
123:             return new Evaluation(false, "HOLD", explanation);
124:         }
125: 
126:         public static Evaluation hold(String code, String explanation) {
127:             return new Evaluation(false, code, explanation);
128:         }
129:     }
130: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/ProfitLockPolicy.java:1-120 =====
1: package com.crypto.position.service;
2: 
3: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
4: 
5: import java.math.BigDecimal;
6: import java.math.RoundingMode;
7: 
8: /**
9:  * Pure Dynamic Profit Lock policy shared by live production and Proven/Regression.
10:  * Keep all percentage/progression math here so replay can never drift from production.
11:  */
12: @Component
13: public class ProfitLockPolicy {
14:     private static final BigDecimal HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
15:     private static final int SCALE = 12;
16: 
17:     public State evaluate(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal target, BigDecimal current,
18:                           BigDecimal previousHighest, boolean previouslyActive, BigDecimal previousLock,
19:                           boolean enabled, BigDecimal activationPercent, BigDecimal initialLockPercent,
20:                           BigDecimal trailStepPercent) {
21:         if (entry == null || target == null || current == null || entry.signum() <= 0 || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) {
22:             return new State(previousHighest, previouslyActive, previousLock, BigDecimal.ZERO, false);
23:         }
24: 
25:         BigDecimal highest = previousHighest;
26:         if (highest == null || highest.compareTo(entry) < 0) highest = entry;
27:         if (current.compareTo(highest) > 0) highest = current;
28:         if (!enabled) return new State(highest, false, null, BigDecimal.ZERO, false);
29: 
30:         BigDecimal distance = target.subtract(entry);
31:         BigDecimal progress = highest.subtract(entry).multiply(HUNDRED)
32:                 .divide(distance, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).max(BigDecimal.ZERO);
33:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(activationPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
34:         BigDecimal initial = nvl(initialLockPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(40));
35:         BigDecimal step = nvl(trailStepPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(10));
36: 
37:         boolean active = previouslyActive;
38:         BigDecimal lock = previousLock;
39:         if (progress.compareTo(activation) >= 0) {
40:             active = true;
41:             BigDecimal completed = progress.subtract(activation).max(BigDecimal.ZERO)
42:                     .divide(step, 0, RoundingMode.DOWN);
43:             BigDecimal lockedProgress = initial.add(completed.multiply(step));
44:             BigDecimal maximum = progress.subtract(step).max(initial);
45:             lockedProgress = lockedProgress.min(maximum).max(initial);
46:             BigDecimal candidate = entry.add(distance.multiply(lockedProgress)
47:                     .divide(HUNDRED, SCALE, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
48:             candidate = candidate.max(entry.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005)));
49:             if (lock == null || candidate.compareTo(lock) > 0) lock = candidate;
50:         }
51:         boolean triggered = active && lock != null && current.compareTo(lock) <= 0;
52:         return new State(highest, active, lock, progress, triggered);
53:     }
54: 
55:     private static BigDecimal nvl(BigDecimal value, BigDecimal fallback) {
56:         return value == null ? fallback : value;
57:     }
58: 
59:     public record State(BigDecimal highestPrice, boolean active, BigDecimal lockPrice,
60:                         BigDecimal progressPercent, boolean triggered) {}
61: }

132:         // earned. The explicit state is now the sole execution authority.

151:

153:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(highest);
154:             position.setProfitLockActive(persistedActive);
155:             position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(lockPrice);
156:             position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
157:             position.setProfitLockActivatedAt(activatedAt);
158:             position.setProfitLockState(state);
159:             position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(rebaseStartedAt);

161:             positionRepository.save(position);
162:         }

165:         boolean hardFloorTriggered = state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
166:                 && current.compareTo(hardProfitFloor) < 0;
167: 
168:         if (changed || triggered || hardFloorTriggered || previousState != state) {
169:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][PROFIT_LOCK_EVAL] positionId={}, symbol={}, current={}, entry={}, target={}, " +
170:                             "previousHighest={}, highest={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousPersistedActive={}, persistedActive={}, " +
171:                             "previousLock={}, lock={}, previousProgressPct={}, progressPct={}, activationPct={}, triggered={}, " +
172:                             "hardFloor={}, hardFloorTriggered={}, persisted={}",
173:                     position.getId(), symbol, current, entry, target,
174:                     previousHighest, highest, previousState, state, previousPersistedActive, persistedActive,
175:                     previousLock, lockPrice, previousProgress, progress, activation, triggered,
176:                     hardProfitFloor, hardFloorTriggered, changed);
177:         }
178: 
179:         String profileText = " Admin profile=" + profile.name() +
180:                 " (activation=" + activation.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%, initial lock=" +
181:                 initialLock.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%, trail=" +
182:                 trailStep.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%).";
183:         String explanation;
184:         if (hardFloorTriggered) {
185:             explanation = "TP-extension rebase is active and price " + current
186:                     + " breached the protected-profit safety floor " + hardProfitFloor + "." + profileText;
187:         } else if (state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
188:             explanation = "Profit Lock is rebasing to the extended take-profit geometry; the historical lock "
189:                     + lockPrice + " remains persisted but is non-executable. Current best progress is "
190:                     + progress.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + "% and reactivation starts at "
191:                     + activation.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%." + profileText;
192:         } else if (triggered) {
193:             explanation = "Price " + current + " reached the protected profit-lock level " + lockPrice
194:                     + " after the position had reached " + progress.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
195:                     + "% of its take-profit distance." + profileText;
196:         } else if (executableActive) {
197:             explanation = "Profit Lock active at " + lockPrice + "; best progress is "
198:                     + progress.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + "% of take-profit distance." + profileText;
199:         } else {
200:             explanation = "Profit Lock not active yet; best progress is "
201:                     + progress.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + "% and activation starts at "
202:                     + activation.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%." + profileText;
203:         }
204: 
205:         return new Evaluation(true, executableActive, triggered, position.getId(), current, highest,
206:                 progress, lockPrice, activation, state, hardProfitFloor, hardFloorTriggered, explanation);
207:     }
208: 
209:     /**
210:      * FIX-120 correction/refinement to FIX-118. Called in the same transaction that persists the new TP.
211:      * A TP change invalidates the prior executable lock as a calculation baseline. If current
212:      * best progress remains at/above activation, stay ACTIVE but derive a fresh lock from the
213:      * new geometry using raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as the historical
214:      * best seed. If progress falls below activation, preserve the historical lock for audit but
215:      * make it non-executable in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE until the current geometry requalifies.
216:      */
217:     public ExtensionTransition onTakeProfitExtended(WalletManagedPosition position, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant changedAt) {
218:         if (position == null || newTarget == null || position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt() == null) {
219:             return ExtensionTransition.none();
220:         }
221:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
222:         BigDecimal oldTarget = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
223:         BigDecimal oldLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
224:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null
225:                 ? position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt()
226:                 : position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
227:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) {
228:             extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
229:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(extensionHighest);
230:         }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionContinuationPolicy.java:1-160 =====
1: package com.crypto.position.service;
2: 
3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
5: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
7: 
8: /**
9:  * Shared production/replay policy for deciding whether a reached profit target should be extended.
10:  *
11:  * IMPORTANT: this class manages an ALREADY-OPEN winner only. It does not loosen BUY generation,
12:  * entry validation, stop loss, or bearish timeframe vetoes.
13:  */
14: @Component
15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
16: public class PositionContinuationPolicy {
17:     /** Minor/moderate immutable-thesis deterioration is tolerated at a TP checkpoint. */
18:     static final int MAX_CONTINUATION_TREND_PRESSURE = 2;
19:     /** Momentum must remain essentially intact for the thesis-preservation continuation path. */
20:     static final int MAX_CONTINUATION_MOMENTUM_PRESSURE = 1;
21: 
22:     private final PositionThesisPressurePolicy thesisPressurePolicy;
23: 
24:     /**
25:      * Backward-compatible evaluator used by older unit fixtures. Production/replay should pass
26:      * the full immutable entry thesis through the overload below.
27:      */
28:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
29:                                Integer entryTrend, Integer entryMomentum, Integer entryVolume) {
30:         return evaluate(current, fiveMinute, oneHour,
31:                 entryTrend, null, entryMomentum, entryVolume, null, null);
32:     }
33: 
34:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
35:                                Integer entryTrend, Integer entryStructure,
36:                                Integer entryMomentum, Integer entryVolume,
37:                                Integer entryConfidence, Integer entryTotal) {
38:         if (current == null) return new Evaluation(false, "Continuation FAIL Aú no fresh signal context.");
39:         if (bearish(current.getDecision()) || (fiveMinute != null && bearish(fiveMinute.getDecision()))
40:                 || (oneHour != null && bearish(oneHour.getDecision()))) {
41:             return new Evaluation(false, "Continuation FAIL Aú bearish timeframe detected; normal exit remains active.");
42:         }
43: 
44:         boolean currentSupport = supportive(current.getDecision());
45:         boolean fiveSupport = fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveMinute.getDecision());
46:         boolean fiveNonBearish = fiveMinute != null && !bearish(fiveMinute.getDecision());
47:         boolean oneSafe = oneHour == null || !bearish(oneHour.getDecision());
48:         boolean oneBullish = oneHour != null && bullish(oneHour.getDecision());
49: 
50:         int trendFloor = Math.max(12, nvl(entryTrend) - 3);
51:         int momentumFloor = Math.max(7, nvl(entryMomentum) - 4);
52:         int volumeSoftFloor = Math.max(2, nvl(entryVolume) - 8);
53: 
54:         boolean trendHealthy = current.getTrendScore() >= trendFloor;
55:         boolean momentumHealthy = current.getMomentumScore() >= momentumFloor;
56:         boolean volumeSupportive = current.getVolumeScore() >= volumeSoftFloor;
57: 
58:         // Standard continuation: structure is still supportive. Volume is deliberately a soft
59:         // confirmation because breakout volume commonly cools while a healthy trend continues.
60:         boolean standard = currentSupport && fiveSupport && oneSafe
61:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy
62:                 && (volumeSupportive || oneBullish);
63: 
64:         // Protected HTF continuation: a live 1h BUY may carry a winner through a neutral/cooling
65:         // 5m phase. It never overrides bearish context and still requires healthy 1m trend/momentum.
66:         boolean htfProtected = currentSupport && oneBullish && fiveNonBearish
67:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
68: 
69:         // Healthy consolidation: low volume is deliberately NOT a hard veto for an already
70:         // profitable position. If 1m is still WATCH/BUY, the higher timeframes are non-bearish,
71:         // and trend/momentum remain healthy, cooling volume alone must not force a TP exit.
72:         boolean healthyConsolidation = currentSupport && fiveNonBearish && oneSafe
73:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
74: 
75:         // HTF-supported consolidation: a strong 1h BUY may carry a winner through one neutral
76:         // 1m candle when 5m remains non-bearish. This is position-management-only; supportive()
77:         // is intentionally unchanged so entry rules are not loosened.
78:         boolean currentNonBearish = !bearish(current.getDecision());
79:         boolean htfSupportedConsolidation = currentNonBearish && !currentSupport
80:                 && oneBullish && fiveNonBearish && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
81: 
82:         PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis = thesisPressurePolicy.evaluate(
83:                 entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
84:                 entryConfidence, entryTotal, current);
85: 
86:         // FIX-011 / SOLUSDT 2026-08-19:
87:         // At TP, SOL had 1m WATCH, 5m NEUTRAL, 1h WATCH and momentum improved 13->15.
88:         // The old binary trend floor rejected continuation solely because trend fell 21->16
89:         // (16 < 18), even though PositionManagementService scored the SAME immutable thesis as
90:         // trend pressure 2/8, momentum pressure 0/5 and HOLD. This narrow path aligns both
91:         // components: minor thesis cooling may extend a winner, but any bearish timeframe still
92:         // vetoes above and genuine trend/momentum breakdown still fails these pressure limits.
93:         boolean thesisIntactConsolidation = currentSupport && fiveNonBearish && oneSafe
94:                 && thesis.trendPressure() <= MAX_CONTINUATION_TREND_PRESSURE
95:                 && thesis.momentumPressure() <= MAX_CONTINUATION_MOMENTUM_PRESSURE;
96: 
97:         boolean extend = standard || htfProtected || healthyConsolidation
98:                 || htfSupportedConsolidation || thesisIntactConsolidation;
99:         String path = htfProtected ? "HTF_TREND"
100:                 : (standard ? "STANDARD"
101:                 : (htfSupportedConsolidation ? "HTF_SUPPORTED_CONSOLIDATION"
102:                 : (healthyConsolidation ? "HEALTHY_CONSOLIDATION"
103:                 : (thesisIntactConsolidation ? "THESIS_INTACT_CONSOLIDATION" : "NONE"))));
104:         String checks = "current=" + decision(current)
105:                 + ", 5m=" + decision(fiveMinute)
106:                 + ", 1h=" + decision(oneHour)
107:                 + ", trend=" + current.getTrendScore() + "/" + trendFloor
108:                 + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore() + "/" + momentumFloor
109:                 + ", volume=" + current.getVolumeScore() + "/soft " + volumeSoftFloor
110:                 + ", thesisPressure=" + thesis.trendPressure() + "/8 trend, "
111:                 + thesis.momentumPressure() + "/5 momentum";
112: 
113:         return new Evaluation(extend, extend
114:                 ? "Continuation PASS (" + path + "): " + checks
115:                     + ". Volume is advisory during a healthy winner; cooling volume alone does not end the trend."
116:                 : "Continuation FAIL: " + checks
117:                     + ". Trend/momentum or higher-timeframe structure no longer supports extending the target; allow normal protection/exit logic.");
118:     }
119: 
120:     private int nvl(Integer v) { return v == null ? 0 : v; }
121:     private String decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? "MISSING" : s.getDecision().name(); }
122:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.BUY || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY || d == SignalDecision.WATCH; }
123:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.BUY || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY; }
124:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
125:     public record Evaluation(boolean extendTarget, String explanation) {}
126: }

470:     /**
471:      * Executes a mechanical live-price exit (TP / SL / Profit Lock) without requiring
472:      * a newly generated trade signal. The open wallet position is the source of truth.
473:      */
474:     @Transactional
475:     public boolean executeMechanicalExit(
476:             String symbol,
477:             BigDecimal executionPrice,
478:             String executionReason,
479:             String executionMessage
480:     ) {
481:         String pair = normalizePair(symbol);
482:         if(walletCoordination!=null) { walletCoordination.symbol(pair); }
483:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
484:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN")
485:                 .orElse(null);
486:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
487:         // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
488:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
489: 
490:         if (position == null || position.getId() == null || position.getQuantity() == null
491:                 || position.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) {
492:             return false;
493:         }
494: 
495:         String reason = executionReason == null || executionReason.isBlank()
496:                 ? "MECHANICAL_EXIT"
497:                 : executionReason.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
498:         String key = "POSITION:" + position.getId() + ":" + reason;
499:         if (executionExists(key)) return true;
500: 
501:         String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
502:         if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
503:         WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
504:         BigDecimal quantity = position.getQuantity().min(coin.getQuantity());
505:         if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return false;
506: 
507:         BigDecimal price = positive(executionPrice);
508:         BigDecimal gross = quantity.multiply(price);
509:         BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity);
510:         BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis);
511:         BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized
512:                 .divide(costBasis, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
513:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
514: 
515:         coin.setQuantity(coin.getQuantity().subtract(quantity));
516:         if (coin.getQuantity().signum() == 0) coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(null);
517:         assetRepository.save(coin);
518:         // FIX-037: credit SELL proceeds atomically so a concurrent BUY cannot overwrite them.
519:         if (assetRepository.creditQuantity("USDT", gross) != 1) {
520:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to credit USDT wallet balance");
521:         }
522:         // FIX-037: keep a final post-credit balance snapshot for lambda-based daily-stat updates.
523:         // Using a separate final variable also avoids capturing a reassigned local variable.
524:         final BigDecimal endingUsdt = getOrCreate("USDT").getQuantity();
525: 
526:         position.setQuantity(ZERO);
527:         position.setTotalCostUsdt(ZERO);
528:         position.setAverageEntryPriceUsdt(ZERO);
529:         position.setStatus("CLOSED");
530:         position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
531:         managedPositionRepository.save(position);
532: 
533:         tradeRepository.save(WalletTrade.builder()
534:                 .executionKey(key)
535:                 .symbol(pair)
536:                 .side("SELL")
537:                 .quantity(quantity)
538:                 .priceUsdt(price)
539:                 .grossAmountUsdt(gross)
540:                 .feeUsdt(ZERO)
541:                 .netAmountUsdt(gross)
542:                 .costBasisUsdt(costBasis)
543:                 .realizedPnlUsdt(realized)
544:                 .realizedPnlPercent(realizedPercent)
545:                 .executionType("PAPER_AUTO")
546:                 .executionReason(reason)
547:                 .status("EXECUTED")
548:                 .executedAt(Instant.now())
549:                 .notes("Mechanical live-price position exit")
550:                 .executionMessage(executionMessage == null ? reason + " triggered at " + price : executionMessage)
551:                 .build());
552: 
553:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC))
554:                 .ifPresent(daily -> {
555:                     daily.setEndingUsdt(endingUsdt);
556:                     daily.setEndingPortfolioUsdt(walletService.currentPortfolioValue());
557:                     daily.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
558:                     dailyStatisticsRepository.save(daily);
559:                 });
560:         walletService.captureSnapshot();
561:         return true;
562:     }

40:     public String invalidReason(BigDecimal price,Instant observed,Instant now){
41:         if(price==null || price.signum()<=0)return "INVALID_PRICE";
42:         if(observed==null)return "MISSING_TIMESTAMP";
43:         if(observed.isAfter(now.plusSeconds(futureSkew)))return "FUTURE_TIMESTAMP";
44:         if(observed.isBefore(now.minusSeconds(maxAge)))return "STALE_PRICE";
45:         return null;

47:     /** FIX-153: called in INPUT transaction. Invalid observations never occupy slot 1.
48:      * A duplicate-key error is classified only if the exact canonical key exists; other
49:      * duplicate constraints/errors propagate. JDBC statement errors do not poison a JPA proxy TX. */
50:     public Long recordPending(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observed){
51:         BigDecimal normalizedPrice=normalizePrice(price);
52:         Instant now=referenceNow();String invalid=invalidReason(normalizedPrice,observed,now);
53:         if(invalid!=null){

55:             Long ageMs=observed==null?null:java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis();

57:             return null;

59:         org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder keys=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();
60:         try {
61:             jdbcTemplate.update(connection->{

66:         }catch(org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate){

80:     /** Exact event lineage prevents a caller from completing a different receipt.
81:      * A lost resolver race rolls back the entire effects transaction. Slot 1 is retained. */
82:     public void finish(long id,String symbol,Instant observed,BigDecimal price,String state,String reason){
83:         price=normalizePrice(price);
84:         if(!org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
85:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome requires effects transaction");
86:         if(!java.util.Set.of("APPLIED","NON_CANONICAL").contains(state))throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 invalid effects outcome");
87:         int n=jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status=?,outcome_reason=? WHERE id=? AND source=? AND symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND price=? AND ordering_slot=1 AND delivery_status='PENDING'",state,reason,id,DIRECT_SOURCE,symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),price);
88:         if(n!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome ownership/lineage changed: event="+id);
89:         org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization(){
90:             @Override public void afterCompletion(int status){
91:                 if(status==STATUS_COMMITTED){
92:                     com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count("APPLIED".equals(state)?com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_APPLIED:com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);

96:             }
97:         });

99:     /** Startup and periodic conditional resolution: no replay and no symbol-entry fence.
100:      * The conditional update waits behind an in-flight effect TX and cannot override APPLIED.
101:      * Runtime absence alone is insufficient; candidates must also exceed a configured bound. */
102:     @org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled(initialDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-initial-delay-ms:5000}",fixedDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-delay-ms:5000}")
103:     public void resolvePending(){

105:         try {

107:             var bounded=new JdbcTemplate(java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(jdbcTemplate.getDataSource()));bounded.setQueryTimeout(2);

109:             var rows=bounded.queryForList("SELECT id,symbol,observed_at FROM market_price_event WHERE source=? AND delivery_status='PENDING' AND created_at<? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 100",DIRECT_SOURCE,Timestamp.from(Instant.now().minusSeconds(120)));

34:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,

55:         boolean momentumHealthy = current.getMomentumScore() >= momentumFloor;
56:         boolean volumeSupportive = current.getVolumeScore() >= volumeSoftFloor;

58:         // Standard continuation: structure is still supportive. Volume is deliberately a soft
59:         // confirmation because breakout volume commonly cools while a healthy trend continues.
60:         boolean standard = currentSupport && fiveSupport && oneSafe
61:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy
62:                 && (volumeSupportive || oneBullish);
63: 
64:         // Protected HTF continuation: a live 1h BUY may carry a winner through a neutral/cooling
65:         // 5m phase. It never overrides bearish context and still requires healthy 1m trend/momentum.

67:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;

69:         // Healthy consolidation: low volume is deliberately NOT a hard veto for an already

73:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
74: 
75:         // HTF-supported consolidation: a strong 1h BUY may carry a winner through one neutral

24:VERDICT: APPROVED only if the gap analysis of THIS trade vs implemented rules is complete with file/ID evidence. APPROVED is not Production safety and does not authorize code changes.
39:- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Q3:** Protection did re-evaluate each accepted live price. At the lock breach, however, it does not assess remaining target distance, momentum, or the advisory output; it invokes `PositionExitPolicy` once using the three latest signal labels, then sells if that policy returns exit. Later upside was not information available at the breach.
59:- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The breach policy reads the latest stored labels without validating their freshness or data quality. The hourly `NEUTRAL` signal was the newest closed hourly result, but about 58 minutes old at exit. This is an implemented-rule observation, not evidence that the trade violated that rule.
63:- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The later rise does not show that a hold would have been better. A policy change would need fee/slippage-aware, out-of-sample testing with intrabar ordering, and cannot rely on this single trade.
73:VERDICT: APPROVED
77:I'm keeping my verdict at **APPROVED**. The answers to all three questions hold. But one step in my round-4 proof was wrong, and I've corrected it below. A price at or above take-profit (TP) is handled before the profit lock is ever checked, so the profit-lock log being silent cannot show that TP was never reached. The conclusion still stands on other evidence. I also add facts showing this exit was effectively fixed from the moment of entry.

Binance could theoretically have traded above TP between kline-close observations, because protection consumes kline field `c`, not intrabar high/low: [BinanceKlineService.java](</C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:91>).

The hourly `NEUTRAL` signal was the newest closed hourly result, but about 58 minutes old at exit. This is an implemented-rule observation, not evidence that the trade violated that rule.
60: 
61: - **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The TP-continuation policy can accept non-bearish 1h context, while the breached-lock hold requires at least 1h `WATCH`. Thus a hypothetical TP touch could have been eligible for continuation under different score inputs, while the actual lock breach had to exit. The position never reached that checkpoint, and the required contemporaneous score inputs for that counterfactual are not fully preserved.

200: - [Binance Developer Docs, "WebSocket Streams" (Spot)](https://developers.binance.com/docs/binance-spot-api-docs/web-socket-streams). No publication date shown; fetched this round.
201:   - Finding: the kline stream "push[es] updates to the current klines/candlestick every second", with an update speed of "1000ms for 1s, 2000ms for the other intervals". Each update carries both the close `c` and the high `h`.
202:   - Limitation: the page doesn't say whether a push is skipped when nothing changed. During the hold, SHIB updates arrived 2 s to 59 s apart, which suggests pushes only on change [AI].
203:   - Relevance: protection sees the close at most once every 2 s. A price spike between updates shows up only in `h`, which protection does not use.

208: ## OBJECTIONS
209: **To my own round 4**
210: - **S1. A step in my proof was wrong (the conclusion is not).**
211:   - I argued that because the profit-lock evaluator logged nothing between 20:47:41.566Z and 20:58:09.534Z, no accepted price went above 0.00000542.
212:   - That only rules out 0.00000543. A price at or above TP enters the TP branch and returns at `LivePositionProtectionService.java:170`, before the lock evaluator runs at `:185`, so it would never produce a lock log.
213:   - "No accepted price reached TP" rests instead on five points:
214:     - (a) The whole archive has zero lines matching `(TAKE_PROFIT|TP_EXTENSION).*SHIBUSDT`. Both possible TP outcomes, extending the target or selling, write log lines that include the symbol (`:120, :164, :168`).
215:     - (b) The TP stored on managed position 64 never changed.
216:     - (c) There is no TAKE_PROFIT trade; trade IDs 106 and 107 are consecutive.
217:     - (d) A silent failed sell is ruled out: position quantity is checked at `:93` in the same transaction, and SELL 107 sold the full quantity with no trade in between.
218:     - (e) The protection-failure and pending-resolved counters stayed at 0.
219: - **S2. One claim was right but poorly supported.** I wrote "protection only ever accepted 0.00000541ƒ+'0.00000542ƒ+'0.00000543ƒ+'0.00000542", but before the lock armed, the logs only record new highs, not every price. It is still true, now supported by:
220:   - the lowest 1m low from 20:45Z being 0.00000541 (DBQ-008);
221:   - the new-high logs, which never exceed 0.00000543;
222:   - point S1, which rules out anything at or above TP.
223: - **S3. "The three patterns cover every rejection path" was overstated.** They cover every rejection inside price-event handling, but not messages dropped earlier in the pipeline:
224:   - a WebSocket connection-gate rejection (`MESSAGE_REJECTED`, which doesn't name the symbol);
225:   - a failed callback (`CALLBACK_FAILED`);
226:   - a drain during shutdown (`KLINE_REJECTED_DRAINING`).
227: 
228:   Neither agent searched for these. Gate rejections are ruled out because the gate only ever moves forward (pending ƒ+' admitting ƒ+' revoked) and was still admitting SHIB messages at 20:58:00 and 20:58:43. Drain only happens at shutdown, and the counters show no restart. Failed-callback lines remain unsearched (Unknown 4).
229: - **S4. "105 ms to the SELL" needs qualifying.** That is the timestamp set inside the transaction. The exit was logged and committed at 20:58:43.837/.839Z, 390 ms after the price was observed. Still no material delay.
230:

232: - **C1.** "Below TP ƒ?İ but below the lock" should say "and below the lock". 20:58:09.444Z is when the price was observed; the lock was saved at 20:58:09.534Z.
233: - **C2.** Codex's "silent failed sell" objection is ruled out by S1(d). The only residual is an unrecorded manual wallet edit, with no evidence of one.
234: - **C3.** The "if 12:38 meant noon" hedge is resolved (see Q2).
235: - **C4.** Codex's round-5 point, that the policy doesn't check whether the labels are fresh, is true of the code (`:95-97`). In this trade, though, all three labels were the newest available for their timeframes. Any breach late in an hour will see a 1h label close to an hour old. This describes the implemented rule, not a defect in this trade.
236: - **C5.** Codex missed the same two gaps I corrected in S1 and S3. Its conclusions are unaffected.

240: - The result files display KSA times, although the log policy says raw values are UTC. For example, DBQ-014's 23:58:09.534 is the runtime log's 20:58:09.534Z.
241: - DBQ-008's columns `first_open_utc` and `last_open_utc` are mislabelled; they hold KSA times.
242: - No conclusion depends on any of this.
243: 
244: **Statistics and Replay**
245: - One trade whose price later rose proves nothing about a better rule. Picking it because of what happened afterwards is outcome-selection bias. Any rule change would need multi-symbol, out-of-sample testing that accounts for fees, slippage and price order within each candle.
246: - Exact-price Replay follows the live order and would read the same price events, but it skips the signal-path exits.
247: - Fallback Replay applies target extension and lock holds to signal prices, which production's signal path does not do.
248: - Replay was not run, so I make no parity claim.
249: 
250: **Entry route (outside Q1ƒ?"Q3; not blocking)**
251: - **E1.** The wake-up route bought 1.1 s after 89469's own evaluation said "Wait for fresh evidence to rebuild".
252:   - The route accepts opportunities still marked BUILDING and does not re-check that blocker.
253:   - Its message "after the prior blocker cleared" is fixed text (`ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:446`), not a condition the code verifies.
254:   - This is within the design, since the fresh 5m BUY counts as the new evidence, but the stored explanation overstates what was checked.
255: - **E2.** Risk/reward was 0.877: stop 0.5% below entry, TP 0.44% above. The wake-up route has no minimum risk/reward check; other entry routes require 1.0ƒ?"1.25. This is a design observation, not a rule violation.

## OBJECTIONS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The document’s claim that a silent TP-path failure is ruled out is incorrect. Its cited position check is not sufficient: the later execution method independently has `false` return paths, and the caller silently returns afterward. No retained query shows every relevant `market_price_event` row, its price, outcome, and reason.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Therefore, “TP was never reached by the evidenced protection path” is defensible only as an evidence limitation, not as a confirmed historical fact. A TP-capable accepted observation followed by a non-executed mechanical exit remains unexcluded.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The document’s timestamps have a material audit-quality defect: database result files render KSA values while their metadata says raw values are UTC, and DBQ-008 labels KSA values as UTC. The runtime log supports the stated UTC/KSA timeline, but the database artifacts must not be presented as unambiguous UTC evidence.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current relevant source files are uncommitted, including live protection, price-event handling, and kline ingestion. The document correctly notes no deployed-binary hash or historical configuration snapshot; this prevents source from proving the exact runtime behavior.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Claude reviewed earlier Codex rounds, while the final merged document has no immutable revision identifier or explicit two-agent approval of that same complete text. This fails the required same-revision review gate.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The fee, tick-size, and later-price discussion is appropriately non-causal, but cannot support a correction proposal without historical account fees, slippage, complete price-event data, and out-of-sample Replay/Production-safe validation.
